(G1)
.

∎

Next is the perturbed double-well potential
W_{\ast\mathsf{c}}
arising from spatial dependence of
\mathsf{c}
. The next lemma verifies the functional
\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},\varepsilon}
defined by (
2.12
) that bounds
\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon}
below (
2.9
) is an inhomogeneous, anisotropic Modica-Mortola functional.

Lemma 3.3

(the potential
W_{\ast\mathsf{c}}
)
.

Let
W_{\ast\mathsf{c}}
be defined by (
2.11
).

(i)

W_{\ast\mathsf{c}}\in C(\overline{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R})
,
W_{\ast\mathsf{c}}(\cdot,v)\in C^{1,1}(\overline{\Omega})
for all
v\in\mathbb{R}
,
W_{\ast\mathsf{c}}(x,\cdot)\in C^{1}(\mathbb{R})
for all
x\in\overline{\Omega}
, and
W_{\ast\mathsf{c}}
is nonnegative.

(ii)

W_{\ast\mathsf{c}}(x,v)=0
iff either
x\in\overline{\Omega}\setminus\Omega_{0}
and
v=0
or
x\in\Omega_{0}
and
v\in\left\{0,\phi_{+}\right\}
.

(iii)

W_{\ast\mathsf{c}}(x,v)=\mathsf{c}(x)g(v)-f^{\ast}(v-\mathsf{a})
.

(iv)

W_{\ast\mathsf{c}}(x,v)\geq(\mathsf{c}(x)-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})g(v)
for all
x\in\overline{\Omega}
and
v\in\mathbb{R}
.

Proof.

Combining the previous lemma with the definition (
2.11
) of
W_{\ast\mathsf{c}}
as
{W_{\ast}}
perturbed by the nonnegative function
(x,v)\mapsto(\mathsf{c}(x)-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})g(v)
gives the corresponding properties of
W_{\ast\mathsf{c}}
.

∎

The proofs of
\Gamma
-convergence and phase separation and the derivation of the limiting velocity law are facilitated by a lower bound on the energy density of the Modica-Mortola functionals
\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},\varepsilon}
and
\mathscr{F}_{\varepsilon}
, which arises from the classical Modica-Mortola trick of combining Young’s inequality with the chain rule:

(3.1)

\mathscr{F}_{\varepsilon}[\phi]=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\Omega}{W_{\ast}}(\phi)\,dx+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\int_{\Omega}\left|\nabla\phi\right|_{{\mathsf{A}}(x)}^{2}\,dx\geq\int_{\Omega}\left|\sqrt{2{W_{\ast}}(\phi)}\nabla\phi\right|_{{\mathsf{A}}(x)}\,dx\eqcolon\int_{\Omega}\left|\nabla\psi\right|_{{\mathsf{A}}(x)}\,dx.

Above,
\psi(x)\coloneq F(\phi(x))
for a transformation

(3.2)

F\colon\mathbb{R}\to\left[0,+\infty\right[,\qquad F(v)\coloneq\begin{cases}0&\text{if }v\leq 0,\\
\displaystyle\int_{0}^{v}\sqrt{2{W_{\ast}}(s)}\,ds&\text{if }0<v<\phi_{+},\\
\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\phi_{+}}\sqrt{2{W_{\ast}}(s)}\,ds&\text{if }v\geq\phi_{+}.\end{cases}

Lemma 3.4

(the transformation
F
)
.

The function
F
defined in (
3.2
) is bounded, nondecreasing, Lipschitz, and continuously differentiable on
\mathbb{R}
.

Proof.

Lemma
3.2
shows
{W_{\ast}}
is nonnegative, continuous, and vanishes exactly at
\left\{0,\phi_{+}\right\}
, which implies
F
is nondecreasing and
C^{1}
. That
F
is bounded is immediate from its definition, and that
F^{\prime}
is bounded follows from the continuity of
{W_{\ast}}
on
[0,\phi_{+}]
.
∎

3.2.
A compactness result

We first show

Lemma 3.5

(coercivity)
.

Suppose
(A2)
,
(G2)
,
(F2)
, and
(C1)
are satisfied.

(i)

There exist constants
c,C>0
such that for any
\varepsilon>0
and
(\rho,\phi)

\mathscr{E}_{\varepsilon}[\rho,\phi]\geq\frac{c}{\varepsilon}\Big(\int_{\Omega}\rho^{m}\,dx+\int_{\Omega}\left|\phi\right|^{q}\,dx+\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\Omega}\left|\nabla\phi\right|^{2}\,dx\Big)-\frac{C}{\varepsilon}.

(ii)

Given a positive sequence
\left\{\varepsilon_{n}\right\}
and a well-prepared sequence
\left\{(\rho_{n},\phi_{n})\right\}

(WP)
, there exists a constant
C>0
, independent of both
\left\{\varepsilon_{n}\right\}
and
\left\{(\rho_{n},\phi_{n})\right\}_{n}
, such that

\left\|\rho_{n}\right\|_{L^{m}(\Omega)}\leq C(\overline{\mathscr{G}}\varepsilon_{n})^{1/m}+C\quad\text{and}\quad\left\|\phi_{n}\right\|_{L^{q}(\Omega)}\leq C(\overline{\mathscr{G}}\varepsilon_{n})^{1/q}+C\qquad\forall\,n\in\mathbb{N}.

We recall when
f
and
g
are compatible for phase separation
(F&G)
that
\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon}\coloneq\mathscr{E}_{\varepsilon}+\mathscr{A}_{\varepsilon}
with a positive constant
\mathscr{A}_{\varepsilon}\coloneq\mathsf{a}/\varepsilon
, so
\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon}\geq\mathscr{E}_{\varepsilon}
. Consequently,
\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon}
satisfies the same coercivity inequality as in Part
(i)
.

Proof.

The estimate is trivial if
\mathscr{E}_{\varepsilon}[\rho,\phi]=+\infty
, so suppose otherwise. The coercivity of
f

(F2)
and
g

(G2)
, the positive minimum

\textstyle\mathsf{c}
\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}

of
\mathsf{c}

(C1)
, and uniform ellipticity of
{\mathsf{A}}

(A2)
give

\displaystyle K_{2}R_{2}^{m}+f(u)\geq K_{2}u^{m}\text{ for all }u\geq 0,\quad{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}K_{1}R_{q}^{q}+\mathsf{c}(x)g(v)\geq{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}K_{1}\left|v\right|^{q}\text{ for all }(x,v)\in\overline{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R},

\displaystyle\left|p\right|^{2}_{{\mathsf{A}}(x)}\geq\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 3.33334pt\hskip-3.33334pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\mathsf{A}}$}\hskip-3.33334pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{{\mathsf{A}}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 3.33334pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 3.33334pt\hskip-3.33334pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\mathsf{A}}$}\hskip-3.33334pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{{\mathsf{A}}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 3.33334pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.33334pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{A}}$}\hskip-2.33334pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{{\mathsf{A}}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.66667pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathsf{A}}$}\hskip-1.66667pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{{\mathsf{A}}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}\left|p\right|^{2}\text{ for all }(x,p)\in\overline{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{d},

which provides

\mathscr{E}_{\varepsilon}[\rho,\phi]\geq\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\Omega}\big(K_{2}\rho^{m}-K_{2}R_{2}^{m}\big)-\rho\phi+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\big(K_{1}\left|\phi\right|^{q}-K_{1}R_{1}^{q}\big)\,dx+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\int_{\Omega}\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 3.33334pt\hskip-3.33334pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\mathsf{A}}$}\hskip-3.33334pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{{\mathsf{A}}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 3.33334pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 3.33334pt\hskip-3.33334pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\mathsf{A}}$}\hskip-3.33334pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{{\mathsf{A}}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 3.33334pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.33334pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{A}}$}\hskip-2.33334pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{{\mathsf{A}}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.66667pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathsf{A}}$}\hskip-1.66667pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{{\mathsf{A}}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}\left|\nabla\phi\right|^{2}\,dx.

Then given
0<\delta<{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}K_{1}
, Young’s inequality
uv\leq\frac{1}{(q\delta)^{q^{\prime}/q}q^{\prime}}u^{q^{\prime}}+\delta v^{q}
gives

\geq\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\Omega}\frac{K_{2}}{2}\rho^{m}+\big(\frac{K_{2}}{2}\rho^{m}-\frac{1}{(q\delta)^{q^{\prime}/q}q^{\prime}}\rho^{q^{\prime}}\big)+\big({\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}K_{1}-\delta\big)\left|\phi\right|^{q}\,dx+c\varepsilon\int_{\Omega}\left|\nabla\phi\right|^{2}\,dx-\frac{C}{\varepsilon}\left|\Omega\right|.

Since
m>q^{\prime}
(cf.
(F2)
) and
K_{1},\delta,q,q^{\prime}>0
, the function
\left[0,+\infty\right[\ni u\mapsto\frac{K_{1}}{2}u^{m}-\frac{1}{(q\delta)^{q^{\prime}/q}q^{\prime}}u^{q^{\prime}}
is bounded below, which implies the estimate in
(i)
.

(ii)
: The estimate from
(i)
and well-preparedness
\overline{\mathscr{G}}\coloneq\sup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon_{n}}[\rho_{n},\phi_{n}]<+\infty
give:

\int_{\Omega}\rho_{n}^{m}\,dx+\int_{\Omega}\left|\phi_{n}\right|^{q}\,dx\leq\frac{\varepsilon_{n}}{c}\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon_{n}}[\rho_{n},\phi_{n}]+C\leq C\overline{\mathscr{G}}\varepsilon_{n}+C.

∎

The following shows well-prepared sequences
\left\{(\rho_{n},\phi_{n})\right\}
admit a convergent subsequence, and that the energy
\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon_{n}}[\rho_{n},\phi_{n}]
evaluated along such sequences is eventually no smaller than our candidate for the
\Gamma
-limit evaluated at the limit
(\rho,\phi)
. It also justifies the use of the
L^{1}(\Omega)^{2}
-topology for the
\Gamma
-convergence of
\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon}
. We recall that classical results
[
Ste88
,
FT89
,
Bou90
,
OS91
]
on the
\Gamma
-convergence of Modica-Mortola-type functionals toward the perimeter are also w.r.t. the
L^{1}(\Omega)
-topology, so the fact that it is also the natural choice for
\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon}
is unsurprising in view of the relationship (
2.8
) between it and the Modica-Mortola functional
\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},\varepsilon}
.

Proposition 3.6

(compactness)
.

Make the standard assumptions
(SA)
, suppose
f
and
g
are compatible
(F&G)
, and
\mathsf{c}
satisfies
(C1)
. Let
\left\{\varepsilon_{n}\right\}
be a positive sequence converging to zero and
\left\{(\rho_{n},\phi_{n})\right\}
be well-prepared
(WP)
. There exists a (not relabeled) subsequence,
\rho\in BV(\Omega;\left\{0,\rho_{+}\right\})
, and
\phi\in BV(\Omega;\left\{0,\phi_{+}\right\})
such that

(i)

\rho_{n}\xrightarrow{n\to\infty}\rho
strongly in
L^{s}(\Omega)
for all
1\leq s<m
, weakly in
L^{m}(\Omega)
, and a.e.;

(ii)

\phi_{n}\xrightarrow{n\to\infty}\phi
strongly in
L^{r}(\Omega)
for all
1\leq r<q
, weakly in
L^{q}(\Omega)
, and a.e.;

(iii)

\displaystyle\phi=\frac{\phi_{+}}{\rho_{+}}\rho
a.e. in
\Omega
,
\chi_{\overline{\Omega}\setminus\Omega_{0}}\phi=0
a.e. in
\Omega
, and
\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\rho\,dx=1
;

(iv)

\displaystyle\liminf_{n\to\infty}\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon_{n}}[\rho_{n},\phi_{n}]\geq\mathscr{G}_{0}[\rho,\phi]
.

Remark 3.7

(strong convergence)
.

Whenever a sequence
\left\{(\rho_{n},\phi_{n})\right\}
is well-prepared and one of the sequences
\left\{\rho_{n}\right\}
or
\left\{\phi_{n}\right\}
converges strongly in
L^{1}(\Omega)
to
\rho_{+}\chi_{E}
or
\phi_{+}\chi_{E}
(resp.), then the other sequence converges in
L^{1}(\Omega)
to the appropriate multiple of
\chi_{E}

without passing to a subsequence
. When
g(v)=v^{2}/2
, the penalty coupling
(\rho,\phi)
in the definition (
2.7
) of
\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon}
is
\varepsilon^{-1}\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho)-\rho\phi+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi)\,dx=\varepsilon^{-1}\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{2{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.08888pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.08888pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-0.77777pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-0.77777pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}}(\rho-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,\phi)^{2}\,dx
, so this is obvious. Generally, it follows from the compactness and uniqueness of limit argument we carry out in the following proof. We use this in Section
3.4.3
to deduce the
L^{1}
-convergence of our recovery sequence for
\rho
from that of
\phi
.

Proof.

Set
\psi_{n}(x)\coloneq F(\phi_{n}(x))
. We will show
\left\{\psi_{n}\right\}
is bounded in
BV(\Omega)
. Since
BV(\Omega)
is compactly embedded into
L^{1}(\Omega)
, this will give a strongly convergent subsequence. We will use this to obtain a strongly convergent subsequence of
\left\{\phi_{n}\right\}
.

To obtain an
L^{1}(\Omega)
bound, we note Lemma
3.4
shows
F
is Lipschitz (and vanishes at
0
from its definition (
3.2
)), so
\left|\psi_{n}\right|\leq C\left|\phi_{n}\right|
; it thus suffices to bound
\left\{\phi_{n}\right\}
in
L^{1}(\Omega)
. For this aim, Lemma
3.5
-
(i)
shows
\left\{\phi_{n}\right\}
is bounded in
L^{q}(\Omega)
. Since
q>1
and we work on a bounded domain,
L^{q}(\Omega)\hookrightarrow L^{1}(\Omega)
.

Since
\mathscr{F}_{\varepsilon}[\phi]\leq\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},\varepsilon}[\phi]\leq\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon}[\rho,\phi]
by (
2.9
) and (
2.12
), the Modica-Mortola trick (
3.1
) and uniform ellipticity
(A2)
give

\overline{\mathscr{G}}\geq\mathscr{F}_{\varepsilon_{n}}[\phi_{n}]\geq\int_{\Omega}\left|\nabla\psi_{n}\right|_{{\mathsf{A}}(x)}\,dx\geq\sqrt{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 3.33334pt\hskip-3.33334pt\hbox{$\displaystyle{\mathsf{A}}$}\hskip-3.33334pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{{\mathsf{A}}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 3.33334pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 3.33334pt\hskip-3.33334pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\mathsf{A}}$}\hskip-3.33334pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{{\mathsf{A}}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 3.33334pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.33334pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathsf{A}}$}\hskip-2.33334pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{{\mathsf{A}}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.66667pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathsf{A}}$}\hskip-1.66667pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{{\mathsf{A}}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\int_{\Omega}\left|\nabla\psi_{n}\right|\,dx,

so
\left\{\nabla\psi_{n}\right\}
is bounded in
L^{1}(\Omega)^{d}
. Hence,
\left\{\psi_{n}\right\}
is bounded in
BV(\Omega)
.

We now pass to a not relabled subsequence of
\left\{\psi_{n}\right\}
that converges strongly in
L^{1}(\Omega)
to
\gamma\phi
. We note that
\phi\in BV(\Omega)
by lower semicontinuity of the
BV(\Omega)
-seminorm for this convergence.

We claim
\phi_{n}\to\phi
strongly in
L^{1}(\Omega)
as well. Fix a small
\delta>0
and consider the open neighborhood
V_{\delta}\coloneq\left\{v\in\left[0,+\infty\right[\,:\,\dist\big({v,\left\{0,\phi_{+}\right\}\big)<\frac{\delta}{2}}\right\}
; we claim there exists
C_{\delta}>0
such that

\left|F(v)-\gamma v\right|^{q}\leq C_{\delta}{W_{\ast}}(v)\qquad\forall\,v\in\mathbb{R}\setminus V_{\delta}.

To see the estimate for large
v\in\mathbb{R}\setminus V_{\delta}
, note the function
v\mapsto F(v)-\gamma v
is Lipschitz on
\mathbb{R}
(Lemma
3.4
) so it grows at most linearly, while
{W_{\ast}}
grows at least as fast as the
q
-th power as
\left|v\right|\to+\infty
, where
q>1
, (Lemma
3.2
-
(iv)
). To obtain the estimate for small
v\in\mathbb{R}\setminus V_{\delta}
, first observe
v\mapsto F(v)-\gamma v
and
W_{\ast}
have the same zeros
\left\{0,\phi_{+}\right\}
(recall the definitions of
F
(
3.2
) and
\gamma
(
2.14
), as well as Lemma
3.2
-
(ii)
). Then, choose
C_{\delta}
possibly larger than before according to a compactness argument. Control in
V_{\delta}
is a result of
v\mapsto F(v)-\gamma v
being Lipschitz and the connected components of
V_{\delta}
having diameter smaller than
\delta
:

\forall\,v\in V_{\delta}\qquad\left|F(v)-\gamma v\right|\leq C\delta.

Combining gives

\displaystyle\left\|\psi_{n}-\gamma\phi_{n}\right\|_{L^{q}(\Omega)}^{q}\leq C\left|\Omega\right|\delta^{q}+C_{\delta}\int_{\left\{\phi_{n}\not\in V_{\delta}\right\}}{W_{\ast}}(\phi_{n})\,dx\leq C\left|\Omega\right|\delta^{q}+C_{\delta}\varepsilon_{n}\mathscr{F}_{\varepsilon_{n}}[\phi_{n}]\leq C\left|\Omega\right|\delta^{q}+C_{\delta}\overline{\mathscr{G}}\varepsilon_{n},

and because
\varepsilon_{n}\to 0
,

\limsup_{n\to\infty}\left\|\psi_{n}-\gamma\phi_{n}\right\|_{L^{q}(\Omega)}\leq C\left|\Omega\right|^{1/q}\delta.

Since this holds for all
\delta>0
and the left-hand side is independent of
\delta
, sending
\delta\to 0^{+}
gives
\limsup_{n\to\infty}\left\|\psi_{n}-\gamma\phi_{n}\right\|_{L^{q}(\Omega)}=0
, and then the triangle inequality and embedding
L^{q}(\Omega)\hookrightarrow L^{1}(\Omega)
give
\phi_{n}\to\phi
in
L^{1}(\Omega)
, which shows one of the modes of convergence in
(ii)
.

By passing to a further subsequence, we also have
\phi_{n}\to\phi
a.e. Then continuity of
{W_{\ast}}
(Lemma
3.2
-
(i)
) gives
{W_{\ast}}\circ\phi_{n}\to{W_{\ast}}\circ\phi
a.e., so Fatou’s lemma and well-preparedness provide

\int_{\Omega}{W_{\ast}}(\phi)\,dx\leq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}{W_{\ast}}(\phi_{n})\,dx\leq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\big(\varepsilon_{n}\mathscr{F}_{\varepsilon_{n}}[\phi_{n}]\big)\leq\overline{\mathscr{G}}\liminf_{n\to\infty}\varepsilon_{n}=0.

Since
{W_{\ast}}
is nonnegative, we deduce
{W_{\ast}}(\phi)=0
a.e., so there exists a measurable set
E\subset\Omega
such that
\phi=\phi_{+}\chi_{E}
since
\left\{{W_{\ast}}=0\right\}=\left\{0,\phi_{+}\right\}
. Continuity of
g
, Fatou’s lemma, and well-preparedness similarly give

\int_{\Omega}(\mathsf{c}-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})g(\phi)\,dx\leq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}(\mathsf{c}(x)-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})g(\phi_{n})\,dx\leq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\big(\varepsilon_{n}\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon_{n}}[\phi_{n}]\big)\leq\overline{\mathscr{G}}\liminf_{n\to\infty}\varepsilon_{n}=0,

and since
(\mathsf{c}-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})g(\phi)
is nonnegative we must have
0=(\mathsf{c}-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})g(\phi)=(\mathsf{c}-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})g(\phi_{+})\chi_{E}
a.e. Since
g(\phi_{+})>0
, this provides the complementarity condition in
(iii)
.

At this stage, we may have
\left|E\right|=0
, but this is resolved by the two remaining equalities in
(iii)
.

As mentioned in the remark preceding this proof, the well-preparedness of
\left\{(\rho_{n},\phi_{n})\right\}
and strong
L^{1}
-convergence of
\left\{\phi_{n}\right\}
to
\phi=\phi_{+}\chi_{E}
imply
\left\{\rho_{n}\right\}
converges strongly in
L^{1}
to
\rho\coloneq\rho_{+}\chi_{E}

without passing to a further subsequence
. To establish this, we will show every subsequence of the current subsequence
\left\{(\rho_{n},\phi_{n})\right\}
admits a further subsequence converging strongly in
L^{1}
to
(\rho,\phi)
. For this purpose, we now pass to an arbitrary (not relabeled) subsequence of
\left\{(\rho_{n},\phi_{n})\right\}
.

Lemma
3.5
-
(ii)
implies
\left\{\rho_{n}\right\}
is bounded in
L^{m}(\Omega)
, so by passing to a subsequence,
\rho_{n}\rightharpoonup\tilde{\rho}
weakly in
L^{m}(\Omega)
. Testing this convergence with nonnegative functions and constant functions implies
\tilde{\rho}\geq 0
a.e. and
\int_{\Omega}\tilde{\rho}\,dx=1
, resp.

We show
\phi=\frac{\phi_{+}}{\rho_{+}}\tilde{\rho}
, which shows
\tilde{\rho}=\rho
a.e. as well as
\left|E\right|>0
in view of
\int_{\Omega}\tilde{\rho}\,dx=1
; this will complete the proof of
(iii)
. To establish this identity, we show
[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\tilde{\rho})-\tilde{\rho}\phi+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi)=0
a.e. Well-preparedness provides

(3.3)

0\leq\limsup_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{n})-\rho_{n}\phi_{n}+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{n})\,dx\leq\limsup_{n\to\infty}\big(\varepsilon_{n}\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon_{n}}[\rho_{n},\phi_{n}]\big)\leq\overline{\mathscr{G}}\lim_{n\to\infty}\varepsilon_{n}=0,

and since the Fenchel-Young inequality implies
[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{n})-\rho_{n}\phi_{n}+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{n})\geq 0
a.e., we have
[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{n})-\rho_{n}\phi_{n}+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{n})\to 0
in
L^{1}(\Omega)
. It suffices to show
\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{n})-\rho_{n}\phi_{n}+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{n})\,dx
is asymptotically no smaller than
\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\tilde{\rho})-\tilde{\rho}\phi+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi)\,dx
.

Combining the uniform
L^{q}(\Omega)
bound on
\left\{\phi_{n}\right\}
from Lemma
3.5
-
(ii)
and the pointwise a.e. convergence from earlier implies the sequence converges weakly in
L^{q}(\Omega)
. We improve the strong convergence by interpolating
L^{p}
-norms and the uniform bound in
L^{q}
, which completes the proof of
(ii)
.

Since
m>q^{\prime}
, we have
m^{\prime}<q
. Hence,
\phi_{n}\to\phi
strongly in
L^{m^{\prime}}(\Omega)
, and since
\rho_{n}\rightharpoonup\tilde{\rho}
weakly in
L^{m}(\Omega)
we can conclude

(3.4)

\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}\rho_{n}\phi_{n}\,dx=\int_{\Omega}\tilde{\rho}\phi\,dx.

The strict convexity and smoothness of
g
imply the same properties of
g^{\ast}
, which then hold for the rescaled function
[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(u)={\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g^{\ast}(u/{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})
. Hence, each of the functionals
\varphi\mapsto\int_{\Omega}{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\varphi)\,dx
and
\mu\mapsto\int_{\Omega}\big[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g\big]^{\ast}(\mu)\,dx
is weakly sequentially lower semicontinuous for
L^{1}(\Omega)
, and thus also for
L^{p}(\Omega)
with
1\leq p<+\infty
since
\Omega
is bounded. Therefore,

(3.5)

\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\tilde{\rho})\,dx\leq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{n})\,dx\quad\text{and}\quad\int_{\Omega}{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi)\,dx\leq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{n})\,dx.

Combining (
3.3
), (
3.4
), and (
3.5
) shows

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{n})-\rho_{n}\phi_{n}+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{n})\,dx

\displaystyle\geq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{n})\,dx-\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}\rho_{n}\phi_{n}\,dx+\liminf_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{n})\,dx\geq\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\tilde{\rho})-\tilde{\rho}\phi+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi)\,dx\geq 0.

Thus, the inequalities are all equalities, and since the final integrand is nonnegative (again by the Fenchel-Young inequality), we conclude

[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\tilde{\rho})-\tilde{\rho}\phi+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi)=0\text{ a.e.\ in }\Omega.

Recall
\phi=\phi_{+}\chi_{E}
, so for
x\in E
we deduce
\tilde{\rho}(x)={\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g^{\prime}(\phi_{+})=\rho_{+}
from the definition of
\phi_{+}
(Lemma
3.2
-
(ii)
) and the identity
{[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}}^{\prime}=({\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g^{\prime})^{-1}
. Similarly, if
x\in\Omega\setminus E
, we have
\tilde{\rho}(x)={\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g^{\prime}(0)=0
. Hence,
\tilde{\rho}=\rho_{+}\chi_{E}\eqcolon\rho=\frac{\rho_{+}}{\phi_{+}}\phi
a.e., and since
\int_{\Omega}\tilde{\rho}\,dx=1
, we see
\left|E\right|=1/\rho_{+}
. The complementarity condition
\chi_{\overline{\Omega}\setminus\Omega_{0}}\chi_{E}=0
a.e. from
(iii)
then implies
\rho_{+}\left|E\cap\Omega_{0}\right|=1
, which is only possible by
(C1)
. This completes the proof of
(iii)
. Now, since
\phi\in BV(\Omega)
and
\phi,\rho
are scalar multiples of one another,
\rho\in BV(\Omega)
.

Having established that a sub-subsequential limit of the density must be
\rho=\rho_{+}\chi_{E}
, where the set
E
was determined by the limit
\phi=\phi_{+}\chi_{E}
of
\left\{\phi_{n}\right\}
, our next aim is to show that the density converges strongly. In previous works on phase separation for PKS systems where
g(v)=v^{2}/2
, the penalty
\varepsilon^{-1}\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho)-\rho\phi+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi)\,dx
in
\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon}
evaluates to
\varepsilon^{-1}\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{2{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.08888pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.08888pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-0.77777pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-0.77777pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}}\rho^{2}-\rho\phi+\frac{{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.08888pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.08888pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-0.77777pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-0.77777pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}}{2}\phi^{2}\,dx=\varepsilon^{-1}\int_{\Omega}\frac{1}{2{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.08888pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.08888pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-0.77777pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-0.77777pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}}(\rho-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\phi)^{2}\,dx
. Thus, the
L^{2}(\Omega)
-distance between
\rho
and
{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,\phi
is a term in
\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon}
, allowing to easily deduce
L^{1}(\Omega)
-convergence of
\left\{\rho_{n}\right\}
from that of
\left\{\phi_{n}\right\}
. In our setting, this penalty and the convergence (
3.3
) will still allow us to deduce the strong convergence of
\left\{\rho_{n}\right\}
. Since we already know
\left\{\rho_{n}\right\}
and
\left\{\phi_{n}\right\}
converge to the appropriate (multiples of) characteristic functions, the left-hand side of (
3.3
) measures simultaneously how much each of
\rho_{n}
and
\phi_{n}
deviate from their limits. We would like to use the integral
\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{n})-\rho_{n}\phi+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi)\,dx
to control how much only the sequence
\left\{\rho_{n}\right\}
deviates from
\rho
. Consequently, we first show the convergence of
\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{n})\,dx
. We use that
[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho)-\rho\phi+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi)=0
a.e. to write

\displaystyle 0\leq\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip By (
3.3
), the integral on the first line vanishes as

, so the second line vanishes, too. Moreover, since

weakly in

and
\phi_{n}\to\phi
in

, the third integral on the second line vanishes. Rearranging, taking the limit

, and using lower semicontinuity (

) shows

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{n})-\rho_{n}\phi_{n}+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{n})\,dx+\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}\rho_{n}\phi_{n}-\rho\phi\,dx

2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi)\,dx\geq 0,

so the inequalities are equalities. Because each
\liminf
is nonnegative (
3.5
), we deduce each is zero. Passing to a subsequence that achieves the first
\liminf
proves what was aimed.

We now show the strong
L^{1}(\Omega)
-convergence of the density. We first define
G(u)\coloneq F(\frac{\phi_{+}}{\rho_{+}}u)
, which has the property
G(\rho)=F(\phi)
a.e., and we will rewrite the limiting density using the identities
\rho=\frac{\rho_{+}}{\phi_{+}}\phi=\frac{\rho_{+}}{\phi_{+}\gamma}F(\phi)
. (Recall, the definitions of
\gamma
, (
2.14
), and
F
, (
3.2
), imply
\gamma=\frac{1}{\phi_{+}}F(\phi_{+})
.) We split the distance

\left|\rho_{n}-\rho\right|\leq\left|\rho_{n}-\frac{\rho_{+}}{\gamma\phi_{+}}G(\rho_{n})\right|+\frac{\rho_{+}}{\gamma\phi_{+}}\left|G(\rho_{n})-F(\phi)\right|.

•

For the first term, notice the Lipschitz function
\left[0,+\infty\right[\ni u\mapsto u-\frac{\rho_{+}}{\gamma\phi_{+}}G(u)
vanishes exactly at
\left\{0,\rho_{+}\right\}
, which are precisely the wells of
W
. Arguing as before with a small neighborhood of these wells
U_{\delta}\coloneq\left\{u\in\left[0,+\infty\right[\,:\,\dist(u,\left\{0,\rho_{+}\right\})<\frac{\delta}{2}\right\}
, the Lipschitz continuity of
u-\frac{\rho_{+}}{\gamma\phi_{+}}G(u)
and superlinearity of
W
(Lemma
3.1
-
(iii)
) provide a constant
C_{\delta}>0
(depending only on
\delta,\frac{\rho_{+}}{\gamma\phi_{+}}G,W
) such that

\displaystyle\left\|\rho_{n}-\frac{\rho_{+}}{\gamma\phi_{+}}G(\rho_{n})\right\|_{L^{1}(\Omega)}\leq C\left|\Omega\right|\delta+C_{\delta}\int_{\left\{\rho_{n}\not\in U_{\delta}\right\}}W(\rho_{n})\,dx\leq C\delta+C_{\delta}\varepsilon_{n}\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon_{n}}[\rho_{n},\phi_{n}]\leq C\delta+C_{\delta}\overline{\mathscr{G}}\varepsilon_{n}.

Sending
n\to\infty
and then
\delta\to 0^{+}
shows this term vanishes.

•

For the term
G(\rho_{n})-F(\phi)
, we recall
\phi=\phi_{+}\chi_{E}
. If
x\in E
, this function evaluates to
G(\rho_{n}(x))-F(\phi_{+})
, and we are lead to consider the function
\left[0,+\infty\right[\ni u\mapsto G(u)-F(\phi_{+})
. It is Lipschitz and vanishes precisely at
\rho_{+}
, which is the unique zero of the continuous, nonnegative function
\left[0,+\infty\right[\ni u\mapsto[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(u)-u\phi_{+}+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{+})
. This latter function is superlinear at
u\to+\infty
since
g^{\ast}
is, and consequently for each small
\delta>0
there exists a constant
C_{\delta}^{\prime}
such that
