\displaystyle\left\|\phi(t_{2})-\phi(t_{1})\right\|_{L^{p}(\Omega)}\leq\left\|\phi(t_{2})-\phi(t_{1})\right\|_{L^{1}(\Omega)}^{1-\theta}\left\|\phi(t_{2})-\phi(t_{1})\right\|_{L^{p_{2}}(\Omega)}^{\theta}\leq\left|\phi\right|_{C^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{1-\theta}\left|\Omega\right|^{\frac{\theta}{p_{2}}}(2\phi_{+})^{\theta}\left|t_{2}-t_{1}\right|^{\frac{1-\theta}{2}}.

The second inequality follows from
\phi\in C^{\frac{1}{2}}([0,T];L^{1}(\Omega))
and the
L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{\infty}(\Omega))
-bound on
\phi
.

That the limit is concentrated on
\Omega_{0}
uses analogous reasoning. For fixed
t\in[0,T]
, continuity of
g
gives
g(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t))\to g(\phi(t))
a.e. in
\Omega
, so nonnegativity of
(\mathsf{c}-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})g(\phi(t))
, Fatou’s lemma, energy dissipation, and well-preparedness give

\displaystyle 0\leq\int_{\Omega}(\mathsf{c}-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})g(\phi(t))\,dx\leq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}(\mathsf{c}-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})g(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t))\,dx\leq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\big(\varepsilon_{n}\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon_{n}}[\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t),\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}]\big)\leq{\overline{\mathscr{G}}}\liminf_{n\to\infty}\varepsilon_{n}=0.

Then nonnegativity of the integrand on the left-hand side provides
(\mathsf{c}-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})\chi_{E(t)}=0
a.e. since
g(\phi(t))=g(\phi_{+})\chi_{E(t)}
with
g(\phi_{+})>0
. Hence, for each
t\in[0,T]
we have
\chi_{\overline{\Omega}\setminus\Omega_{0}}\chi_{E(t)}=0
a.e. in
\Omega
.

From Lemma
4.1
-
(iii)
, up to a subsequence we have

(4.9)

\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}\to\rho\text{ weakly-}\ast\text{ in }L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{m}(\Omega)),

so the nonnegativity and unit mass conditions of
\left\{\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}\right\}
pass to the limit.

We now turn to showing
\rho=(\rho_{+}/\phi_{+})\phi
a.e. in
\left]0,T\right[\times\Omega
. It suffices to show

(4.10)

\int_{0}^{T}\!\!\!\!\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho)-\rho\phi+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi)\,dx\,dt=0.

since the integrand is nonnegative. The energy dissipation inequality and well-preparedness provide

(4.11)

\lim_{n\to\infty}\big([{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{n})-\rho_{n}\phi_{n}+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{n})\big)=0\text{ strongly in }L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{1}(\Omega)).

Let
r=(1-\theta)\cdot 1+\theta q
with
\theta\in\left]0,1\right[
. Then, for a.e.
t\in[0,T]
, we have by interpolating
L^{p}(\Omega)
-norms

\displaystyle\left\|\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t,\cdot)-\phi(t,\cdot)\right\|_{L^{r}(\Omega)}\leq\left\|\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t,\cdot)-\phi(t,\cdot)\right\|_{L^{1}(\Omega)}^{1-\theta}\left\|\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t,\cdot)-\phi(t,\cdot)\right\|_{L^{q}(\Omega)}^{\theta}

Then the uniform-in-
n

L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{q}(\Omega))
-bound (Lemma
4.3
) and the
L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{1}(\Omega))
-convergence imply

(4.12)

\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\|\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}-\phi\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{r}(\Omega))}=0\qquad\forall r\in\left[1,q\right[.

Since
m>q^{\prime}>1
, we have
m^{\prime}<q
, so combining the above strong convergence with the weak-
\ast
convergence in (
4.9
) gives

\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{0}^{T}\!\!\!\!\int_{\Omega}\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}\,dx\,dt=\int_{0}^{T}\!\!\!\!\int_{\Omega}\rho\phi\,dx\,dt.

The functionals
\varphi\mapsto\int_{0}^{T}\!\!\int_{\Omega}{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\varphi)\,dx\,dt
and
\mu\mapsto\int_{0}^{T}\!\!\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\mu)\,dx\,dt
are weakly sequentially lower semicontinuous for, say,
L^{q}(\left]0,T\right[\times\Omega)
and
L^{m}(\left]0,T\right[\times\Omega)
, resp., so

\displaystyle\liminf_{n\to\infty}\int_{0}^{T}\!\!\!\!\int_{\Omega}{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})\,dx\,dt\geq\int_{0}^{T}\!\!\!\!\int_{\Omega}{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi)\,dx\,dt,\quad\liminf_{n\to\infty}\int_{0}^{T}\!\!\!\!\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}})\,dx\,dt\geq\int_{0}^{T}\!\!\!\!\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho)\,dx\,dt.

Then using the same argument as in Proposition
3.6
provides (
4.10
), which provides (
4.7
) (we omit the details since they are the same as in Proposition
3.6
). In particular, we have proven
\rho=(\rho_{+}/\phi_{+})\phi
a.e.

We show the density converges in
L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{1}(\Omega))
. This can be done by arguing pointwise in time as in the time-independent case of the compactness result (Proposition
3.6
) provided the convergence of the contributions to
\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon}
from the reaction terms is essentially uniform-in-time. We point out that the following argument uses 1) some uniform-in-time convergence we have already established for
\left\{\phi_{n}\right\}
(cf. (
4.12
)) and
\left\{\rho_{n}\right\}
(Proposition
4.2
-
(i)
) and 2) that
m>q^{\prime}
(cf.
(G2)
,
(F2)
).

Claim 1
.

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\|\int_{\Omega}\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(\cdot,x)\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(\cdot,x)-\rho(\cdot,x)\phi(\cdot,x)\,dx\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T)}=0
.

Proof of claim.

Fix
t\in[0,T]
and let
\phi_{\delta}(t,x)\coloneq(\eta_{\delta}\ast\phi(t))(x)
, where
\eta_{\delta}
is the standard mollifier for the spatial variable. Then it is easy to show, using that
\phi\in C^{1/4}([0,T];L^{m^{\prime}}(\Omega))
, that
\phi_{\delta}\in C^{1/4}([0,T];L^{m^{\prime}}(\Omega))\cap L^{\infty}(0,T;W^{\frac{2m}{m-1}}(\Omega))
, and
\phi_{\delta}\to\phi
in
C([0,T];L^{m^{\prime}}(\Omega))
. Then we split

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \left|\int_{\Omega}\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)-\rho(t)\phi(t)\,dx\right|&\leq\left|\int_{\Omega}(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)-\phi(t))(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)-\rho(t))\,dx\right|+\left|\int_{\Omega}(\phi(t)-\phi_{\delta}(t))(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)-\rho(t))\,dx\right|\\
&\qquad+\left|\int_{\Omega}\phi_{\delta}(t)(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)-\rho(t))\,dx\right|\qquad+\left|\int_{\Omega}(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)-\phi(t))\rho(t)\,dx\right|\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\leq\left\|\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}-\phi\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{m^{\prime}}(\Omega))}(\left\|\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}-\rho\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{m}(\Omega))}+\left\|\rho\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{m}(\Omega))})\\
&\qquad+\left\|\phi-\phi_{\delta}\right\|_{C([0,T];L^{m^{\prime}}(\Omega))}\left\|\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}-\rho\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{m}(\Omega))}+\left\|\phi_{\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;W^{1,\frac{2m}{m-1}}(\Omega))}\left\|\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}-\rho\right\|_{C([0,T];(W^{1,\frac{2m}{m-1}}(\Omega))^{\ast})}.\end{aligned}

Recall
\left\{\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}\right\}
is bounded in
L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{m}(\Omega))
. Then, we take the essential supremum over
t
, send
n\to\infty
using (
4.12
) (recall
m^{\prime}<q
) and Proposition
4.2
-
(i)
, and then send
\delta\to 0^{+}
, which proves the claim.
∎

Claim 2
.

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\|\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(\cdot,x))-[\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho(\cdot,x))\,dx\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T)}=0
.

Proof of claim.

We show

(4.13)

\begin{gathered}0\leq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\left[\essinf_{t\in[0,T]}\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t))-[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho(t))\,dx\right]\leq\limsup_{n\to\infty}\left[\esssup_{t\in[0,T]}\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t))-[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho(t))\,dx\right]\leq 0.\end{gathered}

The second inequality is obvious, so the task is to show the first and third inequalities.

Step 1:
At a.e. fixed time
t\in[0,T]
we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t))-\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t))\,dx+\int_{\Omega}\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)-\rho(t)\phi(t)\,dx

(4.14)

\displaystyle=\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t))-[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho(t))\,dx+\int_{\Omega}{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi(t))\,dx,

where the second line follows from the equality
[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho)-\rho\phi+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi)=0
a.e. in
\left]0,T\right[\times\Omega
. The first line converges to
0
in
L^{\infty}(0,T)
by (
4.11
) and Claim
1
.

Step 2:
In (
4.14
), if we obtain an essentially-uniform-in-
t
asymptotically negligible lower bound on one integral, then we obtain an essentially-uniform-in-
t
asymptotically negligible upper bound on the other integral. This allows to show the last inequality in (
4.13
).

The first-order convexity inequality for
g
gives, for a.e.
t\in[0,T]
,

\int_{\Omega}{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t))-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi(t))\,dx\geq\int_{\Omega}{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g^{\prime}(\phi(t))(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)-\phi(t))\,dx=\int_{\Omega}\rho(t)(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)-\phi(t)).

We use Hölder’s inequality, take the essential infimum over
t\in[0,T]
, and use (
4.12
) to deduce

(4.15)

\begin{gathered}\liminf_{n\to\infty}\left[\essinf_{t\in[0,T]}\int_{\Omega}g(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t))-g(\phi(t))\,dx\right]\geq-\left\|\rho\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{m}(\Omega))}\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\|\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}-\phi\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{m^{\prime}}(\Omega))}=0,\end{gathered}

so combining with the identity in Step 1, (
4.11
), and Claim
1
gives the last inequality in (
4.13
).

Step 3:
By the first-order convexity inequality, we obtain for a.e.
t\in[0,T]

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t))-[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho(t))\,dx\geq\int_{\Omega}{g^{\ast}}^{\prime}(\rho(t)/{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)-\rho(t))\,dx=\int_{\Omega}\phi(t)(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)-\rho(t))\,dx.

As in the proof of Claim
1
, we regularize
\phi\in C^{1/4}([0,T];L^{m^{\prime}}(\Omega))
to some

\phi_{\delta}\in C^{1/4}([0,T];L^{m^{\prime}}(\Omega))\cap L^{\infty}(0,T;W^{1,\frac{2m}{m-1}}(\Omega))
such that
\phi_{\delta}\to\phi
in
C([0,T];L^{m^{\prime}}(\Omega))
. Then we can bound the right-hand side from below by

\begin{gathered}\geq-\left\|\phi_{\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;W^{1,\frac{2m}{m-1}}(\Omega))}\left\|\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}-\rho\right\|_{C([0,T];W^{1,\frac{2m}{m-1}}(\Omega)^{\ast})}-\left\|\phi-\phi_{\delta}\right\|_{C([0,T];L^{m^{\prime}}(\Omega))}\left\|\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}-\rho\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{m}(\Omega))}.\end{gathered}

Using the
L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{m}(\Omega))
bound on
\left\{\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}\right\}
, we obtain a lower bound on the second term. We then send
n\to\infty
, apply Proposition
4.2
-
(i)
, and then send
\delta\to 0^{+}
, which gives

\liminf_{n\to\infty}\left[\essinf_{t\in[0,T]}\int_{\Omega}[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t))-[{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho(t))\,dx\right]\geq 0.

This proves the first inequality in (
4.13
) and thus proves the claim.

∎

The same argument for the strong convergence of the density in Proposition
3.6
can be carried out pointwise a.e.
t\in[0,T]
, and the resulting upper bounds vanish in
L^{\infty}(0,T)
due to Claims
1
and
2
. This provides
\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}\to\rho
in
L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{1}(\Omega))
. By interpolating
L^{p}(\Omega)
-norms and combining this convergence with the
L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{m}(\Omega))
-bound on
\left\{\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}\right\}
, we see
\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}\to\rho
in
L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{s}(\Omega))
for all
1\leq s<m
.

∎

5.
Normal velocity of the free boundary
V

5.1.
Equipartition of the energies

We derive some classical consequences of the assumption that
(\rho,\phi)
and
\left\{(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}},\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})\right\}
satisfy the energy convergence condition
(EC)
; see
[
LM89
, Lemma 1]
,
[
CNP10
, proof of Theorem 3.3]

[
LS18
, Lemma 2.11]
,
[
KMW23
, Section 5.2]
,
[
Mel24
, proof of Proposition 5.1]
,
[
LSU24
, Section 4.2]
, or
[
MR24
, Lemma 7.1]
. Namely, we show, in the limit
\varepsilon\to 0^{+}
, the potential and interface penalty in
\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},\varepsilon}
are equal asymptotically, the sum of the potential
\varepsilon^{-1}W(\rho_{\varepsilon})
and penalty
\varepsilon^{-1}\big([{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{\varepsilon})-\rho_{\varepsilon}\phi_{\varepsilon}+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{\varepsilon})\big)
coupling
(\rho_{\varepsilon},\phi_{\varepsilon})
is asymptotically equal to the interface penalty, and the gaps between
\mathscr{F}_{\varepsilon}[\phi_{\varepsilon}]
,
\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},\varepsilon}[\phi_{\varepsilon}]
,
\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon}[\rho_{\varepsilon},\phi_{\varepsilon}]
, and
\mathscr{G}_{0}[\rho,\phi]
vanish. These allows us to show, at a.e. time
t\in[0,T]
, the contributions to the energies converge weakly-
\ast
as Radon measures on
\overline{\Omega}
to (an equal proportion of) the limiting contributor to the limiting energy
\mathscr{G}_{0}
(Lemma
5.1
-
(v)
).

Lemma 5.1

(equipartition of the energies)
.

Let
\left\{(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}},\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})\right\}
be weak solutions of (

P
ε

) with well-prepared initial data
\left\{(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}^{\mathrm{in}},\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}^{\mathrm{in}})\right\}
, i.e., satisfying
(WP)
, for which the conclusion of Proposition
4.4
holds with limit
(\rho,\phi)
. If
(\rho,\phi)
,
\left\{(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}},\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})\right\}
satisfy energy convergence
(EC)
, then up to another subsequence:

(i)

for a.e.
t\in[0,T]
and strongly in
L^{1}(0,T)
,

\displaystyle\mathscr{G}_{0}[\rho,\phi]=\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon_{n}}[\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}},\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}]

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},\varepsilon}[\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}]=\mathscr{F}_{\varepsilon_{n}}[\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}]=\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}\left|\nabla\psi_{\varepsilon_{n}}\right|_{\mathsf{A}}\,dx;

(ii)

for a.e.
t\in[0,T]
and strongly in
L^{1}(0,T)
,
\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{n}}\int_{\Omega}(\mathsf{c}(x)-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})g(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})\,dx=0
;

(iii)

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{n}}\Big(W(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}})+\big([{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}})-\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})\big)-W_{\ast}(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})\Big)=0
strongly in
L^{1}(\left]0,T\right[\times\Omega)
;

(iv)

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\varepsilon_{n}}}\sqrt{W_{\ast}(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})}-\sqrt{\frac{\varepsilon_{n}}{2}}\left|\nabla\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}\right|_{\mathsf{A}}\Big)=0
strongly in
L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega))
and

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{n}}W_{\ast}(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})-\frac{1}{2}\left|\psi_{\varepsilon_{n}}\right|_{\mathsf{A}}\Big)=\lim_{n\to\infty}\Big(\frac{\varepsilon_{n}}{2}\left|\nabla\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}\right|_{\mathsf{A}}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\left|\psi_{\varepsilon_{n}}\right|_{\mathsf{A}}\Big)=0
strongly in
L^{1}(\left]0,T\right[\times\Omega)
;

(v)

for any test function
\zeta\in C(\overline{\Omega})
and a.e.
t\in[0,T]

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma}{2}\int_{\Omega}\zeta(x)\left|\vec{\nu}(t,x)\right|_{{\mathsf{A}}(x)}\,d|\nabla\phi(t)|(x)=\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}\zeta(x)\frac{1}{2}\left|\nabla\psi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t,x)\right|_{{\mathsf{A}}(x)}\,dx

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}\zeta(x)\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{n}}W_{\ast}(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t,x))\,dx=\lim_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}\zeta(x)\frac{\varepsilon_{n}}{2}\left|\nabla\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t,x)\right|_{{\mathsf{A}}(x)}^{2}\,dx.

Remark 5.2

(asymptotic equality)
.

(1)

The limit in
(iii)
expresses asymptotic equality in the Fenchel-Young inequality
f(\rho_{\varepsilon})+f^{\ast}(\phi_{\varepsilon}-a)-\rho_{\varepsilon}(\phi_{\varepsilon}-a)\geq 0
. The formula (
2.8
) for
\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon}
shows this difference in
(iii)
is the density of the gap between
\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon}
and the Modica-Mortola functional
\mathscr{F}_{{\mathsf{c}},\varepsilon}
(cf. (
2.12
)).

(2)

The first limit in
(iv)
is classical and represents asymptotic equality in Young’s inequality. Indeed, expanding the square in the
L^{2}(\Omega)
-norm gives
\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{n}}W_{\ast}(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})+\frac{\varepsilon_{n}}{2}\left|\nabla\phi\right|_{\mathsf{A}}^{2}-\sqrt{2W_{\ast}(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})}\left|\nabla\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}\right|_{\mathsf{A}}
, which is nonnegative by Young’s inequality.

Proof.

Throughout the proof, we will abbreviate the energies as

\displaystyle\mathscr{G}_{n}(t)\coloneq\mathscr{G}_{\varepsilon_{n}}[\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t),\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)],\quad\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},n}(t)\coloneq\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},\varepsilon}[\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)],\quad\mathscr{F}_{n}(t)\coloneq\mathscr{F}_{\varepsilon_{n}}[\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)],\quad\mathscr{G}_{0}(t)\coloneq\mathscr{G}_{0}[\rho(t),\phi(t)].

In this notation, the energy dissipation inequality (Lemma
4.1
-
(i)
) and
(WP)
provide
\mathscr{G}_{n}(t)\leq{\overline{\mathscr{G}}}
for a.e.
t\in[0,T]
and all
n\in\mathbb{N}
. We recall the ordering (see (
2.9
), (
2.12
)) and that applying the Modica-Mortola trick for
\mathscr{F}_{n}(t)
gives

(5.1)

\displaystyle\text{a.e.\ }t\in[0,T]\text{ and each }n\in\mathbb{N}\qquad{\overline{\mathscr{G}}}\geq\mathscr{G}_{n}(t)\geq\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},n}(t)\geq\mathscr{F}_{n}(t)\geq\int_{\Omega}\left|\nabla\psi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)\right|_{{\mathsf{A}}(x)}\,dx\geq 0,

where
\psi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t,x)\coloneq F(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t,x))
with
F
defined by (
3.2
) (see also Lemma
3.4
).

(i)
: For the first equality, we write
\left|\mathscr{G}_{n}(t)-\mathscr{G}_{0}(t)\right|=2(\mathscr{G}_{0}(t)-\mathscr{G}_{n}(t))_{+}-(\mathscr{G}_{0}(t)-\mathscr{G}_{n}(t))
. Then after an integration over
t\in[0,T]
, the second term vanishes as
n\to\infty
by the energy convergence assumption
(EC)
, so it only remains to show the first term vanishes in
L^{1}(0,T)
. The
\liminf
inequality of
\Gamma
-convergence (Theorem
2.1
-
(i)
) implies for a.e.
t\in[0,T]
that
\lim_{n\to\infty}(\mathscr{G}_{0}(t)-\mathscr{G}_{n}(t))_{+}=0
, and the
\liminf
inequality combined with Fatou’s lemma implies
\mathscr{G}_{0}\in L^{1}(0,T)
. By nonnegativity of
\mathscr{G}_{n}
, we have the upper bound
(\mathscr{G}_{0}(t)-\mathscr{G}_{n}(t))_{+}\leq\mathscr{G}_{0}(t)
for a.e.
t\in[0,T]
. Then Lebesgue dominated convergence gives convergence in
L^{1}(0,T)
, so after extracting a subsequence we have

(5.2)

\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathscr{G}_{n}=\mathscr{G}_{0}\text{ a.e.\ }t\in[0,T]\text{ and strongly in }L^{1}(0,T).

To show the remaining equalities in
(i)
, we first prove the convergence pointwise for a.e.
t\in[0,T]
by proving a
\liminf
and
\limsup
inequality, which is made straightforward by (
5.2
) and the ordering (
5.1
).

Since
\psi_{\varepsilon_{n}}\to\gamma\phi
in
C([0,T];L^{1}(\Omega))
(Proposition
4.4
),
\phi\in L^{\infty}(0,T;BV(\Omega))
, and for each
t\in[0,T]
the sequence
\left\{\psi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)\right\}
is bounded in
BV(\Omega)
, we have for each
t\in[0,T]
that
\nabla\psi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)\to\gamma\nabla\phi(t)
weakly-
\ast
as Radon measures on
\overline{\Omega}
. Then (
5.2
), (
5.1
), and Reshetnyak lower semicontinuity
[
AFP00
, Theorem 2.38]
give for a.e.
t\in[0,T]

(5.3)

\begin{gathered}\mathscr{G}_{0}(t)=\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathscr{G}_{n}(t)\geq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},n}(t)\geq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\mathscr{F}_{n}(t)\\
\geq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}\left|\frac{\nabla\psi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t,x)}{\left|\nabla\psi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t,x)\right|}\right|_{{\mathsf{A}}(x)}\left|\nabla\psi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t,x)\right|\,dx\geq\gamma\int_{\Omega}\left|\vec{\nu}(t,x)\right|_{{\mathsf{A}}(x)}d|\nabla\phi(t)|(x)=\mathscr{G}_{0}(t).\end{gathered}

Thus, all of the inequalities are equalities. The corresponding
\limsup
inequalities are trivial; we have from (
5.2
), (
5.1
), and (
5.3
) that for a.e.
t\in[0,T]

(5.4)

\begin{gathered}\mathscr{G}_{0}(t)=\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathscr{G}_{n}(t)\geq\limsup_{n\to\infty}\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},n}(t)\geq\limsup_{n\to\infty}\mathscr{F}_{n}(t)\\
\geq\limsup_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}\left|\nabla\psi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)\right|_{{\mathsf{A}}(x)}\,dx\geq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\int_{\Omega}\left|\nabla\psi_{\varepsilon_{n}}(t)\right|_{{\mathsf{A}}(x)}\,dx\geq\mathscr{G}_{0}(t),\end{gathered}

so the inequalities are equalities again. Then (
5.3
), (
5.4
) show the pointwise a.e. convergence in
(i)
. On the other hand, the upper bound in (
5.1
) provided by energy dissipation and well-preparedness shows each of the energies is dominated on
[0,T]
, so Lebesgue dominated convergence yields
L^{1}(0,T)
convergence, which completes the proof of
(i)
.

(ii)
: Since
(\mathsf{c}-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})g(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})\geq 0
a.e. in
\left]0,T\right[\times\Omega
(recall
(C1)
,
(G1)
), we note from (
2.12
), (
2.11
)

0\leq\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{n}}\int_{\Omega}(\mathsf{c}-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})g(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})\,dx=\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},n}-\mathscr{F}_{n}\xrightarrow{n\to\infty}0,

where the convergence follows from the second and third limits of
(i)
.

(iii)
: The definition of
W_{\ast}
(
2.10
) implies
W(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}})+\big([{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}})-\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})\big)-W_{\ast}(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})\geq 0
a.e. in
\left]0,T\right[\times\Omega
. Then the definition of
\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},n}
(
2.12
), that
\mathscr{G}_{n}(t)\geq\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},n}(t)
(
2.9
), and the first and third limits of
(i)
provide

0\leq\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{n}}\int_{\Omega}W(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}})+\big([{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g]^{\ast}(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}})-\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}}\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}+{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}}\,g(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})\big)-W_{\ast}(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}})\,dx=\mathscr{G}_{n}-\mathscr{F}_{\mathsf{c},n}\xrightarrow{n\to\infty}0.

(iv)
: For clarity, let

\displaystyle u_{n}\coloneq\frac{\varepsilon_{n}}{2}\left|\nabla\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}\right|^{2}_{\mathsf{A}},\qquad v_{n}\coloneq\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{n}}W_{\ast}(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}),

\displaystyle o_{n}\coloneq\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{n}}(\mathsf{c}-{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.1111pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.1111pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}})g(\phi_{\varepsilon_{n}}),\qquad w_{n}\coloneq\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{n}}\Big(W(\rho_{\varepsilon_{n}})+\big([{\mathchoice{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\displaystyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-2.22221pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-2.22221pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\textstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip 2.5pt\hskip-1.55556pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathsf{c}$}\hskip-1.55556pt\hskip-2.5pt\raisebox{-5.42776pt}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\underline{\hphantom{\mathsf{c}}}$}}\hskip-2.5pt\hskip 2.5pt}}{\hbox{\hskip
